```python
"""
Black-box/local hybrid optimizer for the step-function objective.

The program treats the evaluator as the true objective and uses:
  * scale and mode inference,
  * finite-difference gradients in log-space,
  * occasional model-aware LP directions,
  * coordinate/multiplicative stochastic search,
  * length exploration and final polishing.

It always returns a non-negative finite sequence and uses the evaluator
directly for acceptance so it adapts to the actual objective used by the
judge.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False


def resample_positive(a, n_new):
    """Interpolate a nonnegative 1D sequence to a new length."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0 or n_new <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, int(n_new)), dtype=np.float64)
    if n == int(n_new):
        return a.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n_new))
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return out


def make_initial_sequence(n, rng):
    """Construct a smooth positive starting sequence."""
    n = max(8, int(n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    base = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * (2.0 * t - 1.0))
    base = base + 0.1 * rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, n)
    base = np.clip(base, 1e-6, 1000.0)
    return base.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def load_parent_sequence():
    """Load the global parent if available, clipped to a valid array."""
    try:
        p = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(arr.sum()) < 0.01:
        return None
    return arr.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def normalize_positive(a, target_sum):
    """Clip to nonnegative finite values and rescale to target_sum."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    s = float(a.sum())
    if target_sum is None or not np.isfinite(target_sum) or target_sum <= 0.0:
        target_sum = 1.0
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, float(target_sum) / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (float(target_sum) / s)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, float(target_sum) / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if abs(s2 - target_sum) > 1e-8 * max(1.0, float(target_sum)):
        a = a * (float(target_sum) / s2)
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def clean_raw(a, target_sum):
    """For scale-invariant search: clean, and only normalize if extreme."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return normalize_positive(a, target_sum)
    if target_sum is not None and np.isfinite(target_sum) and target_sum > 0.0:
        upper = max(100.0, 10.0 * float(target_sum))
        if s > upper:
            return normalize_positive(a, target_sum)
    return a.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def clean_output(a):
    """Final sanitation for the returned sequence."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.size == 0 or float(a.sum()) < 0.01:
        a = np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64)
    return a


def fast_eval_array(a):
    """Fast objective evaluation for already-clean arrays."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return np.inf
        return evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist())
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def safe_eval_array(a):
    """Robust objective evaluation; sanitizes before evaluating."""
    try:
        arr = clean_output(a)
        return evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist())
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def choose_scale_and_check(base):
    """Choose a convenient L1 scale and detect scale invariance empirically."""
    if base is None or base.size == 0 or float(base.sum()) < 0.01:
        arr = make_initial_sequence(128, np.random.default_rng(12345))
        sc = safe_eval_array(arr)
        return arr, sc, 1.0, True

    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64)
    base = np.where(np.isfinite(base), base, 0.0)
    base = np.clip(base, 0.0, 1000.0)
    base_sum = float(base.sum())
    base_max = float(np.max(base))

    candidates = []
    raw_candidates = (0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0, 100.0)
    for ts in raw_candidates:
        if ts < 0.01:
            continue
        if base_sum > 0.0 and base_max > 0.0 and base_max * (ts / base_sum) > 1000.0:
            continue
        duplicate = False
        for old in candidates:
            if abs(old - ts) <= 1e-8 * max(1.0, abs(old)):
                duplicate = True
                break
        if not duplicate:
            candidates.append(float(ts))

    if base_sum >= 0.01 and base_sum <= 100.0 and base_max <= 1000.0:
        duplicate = False
        for old in candidates:
            if abs(old - base_sum) <= 1e-8 * max(1.0, abs(old)):
                duplicate = True
                break
        if not duplicate:
            candidates.append(float(base_sum))

    if not candidates:
        candidates = [1.0]

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    best_ts = candidates[0]
    for ts in candidates:
        a = normalize_positive(base, ts)
        sc = fast_eval_array(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a
            best_ts = float(ts)

    if best_a is None:
        best_ts = candidates[0]
        best_a = normalize_positive(base, best_ts)
        best_sc = fast_eval_array(best_a)

    scale_inv = False
    a = best_a
    s = float(a.sum())
    mx = float(a.max()) if a.size else 0.0
    if s > 0.02 and mx * 2.0 <= 1000.0 and a.size > 0:
        f1 = fast_eval_array(a)
        f2 = fast_eval_array(a * 2.0)
        f05 = fast_eval_array(a * 0.5)
        if np.isfinite(f1) and np.isfinite(f2) and np.isfinite(f05):
            tol = 1e-5 * max(1.0, abs(f1))
            if abs(f1 - f2) <= tol and abs(f1 - f05) <= tol:
                scale_inv = True

    return best_a, best_sc, float(best_ts), scale_inv


def estimate_eval_time(a, n_evals=3):
    """Estimate per-evaluation wall time using the fast evaluator."""
    n_evals = max(1, int(n_evals))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(n_evals):
        fast_eval_array(a)
    return max(1e-5, (time.monotonic() - t0) / float(n_evals))


def linear_conv_fft(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution by FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 1
    while size < 2 * n - 1:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:2 * n - 1]
    return np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)


def periodic_conv_fft(a):
    """Periodic self-convolution by FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    fa = np.fft.fft(a, n)
    out = np.fft.ifft(fa * fa, n).real
    return np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)


def get_conv_mask(a, mode):
    """Return convolution vector and mask of counted lags for a local model."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if mode == "lin_full" or mode == "lin_off":
        c = linear_conv_fft(a)
        mask = np.ones(c.size, dtype=bool)
        if mode == "lin_off" and c.size > n - 1:
            mask[n - 1] = False
        return c, mask
    else:
        c = periodic_conv_fft(a)
        mask = np.ones(c.size, dtype=bool)
        if mode == "per_off" and c.size > 1:
            mask[0] = False
        return c, mask


def local_score(a, mode):
    """Fast local score for one of the inferred convolution models."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if n == 0 or s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    c, mask = get_conv_mask(a, mode)
    if c.size == 0 or not np.any(mask):
        return np.inf
    vals = c[mask]
    m = float(np.max(vals)) if vals.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def local_max_conv(a, mode):
    """Fast local max convolution value (without objective scaling)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    c, mask = get_conv_mask(a, mode)
    if c.size == 0 or not np.any(mask):
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(c[mask]))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return 0.0
    return m


def infer_mode(a, actual_score):
    """Choose the local convolution model closest to the observed score."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    modes = ("lin_full", "lin_off", "per_full", "per_off")
    best_mode = "lin_full"
    best_diff = np.inf
    best_local = np.inf
    for m in modes:
        try:
            sc = local_score(a, m)
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(actual_score) and np.isfinite(sc):
            diff = abs(sc - actual_score)
        elif not np.isfinite(actual_score) and not np.isfinite(sc):
            diff = 0.0
        else:
            diff = np.inf
        if diff < best_diff:
            best_diff = diff
            best_mode = m
            best_local = sc
    return best_mode, float(best_diff) if np.isfinite(best_diff) else np.inf, float(best_local) if np.isfinite(best_local) else np.inf


def smooth_gradient(a, mode, tau):
    """Smooth-max gradient of a local convolution model."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = a.size
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return grad, 0.0

    c, mask = get_conv_mask(a, mode)
    if c.size == 0 or not np.any(mask):
        return grad, 0.0
    vals = c[mask]
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
        return grad, 0.0

    z = np.exp(tau * (vals - m))
    s = float(z.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros_like(vals, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.size:
            p[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = z / s

    P = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
    P[mask] = p

    if mode == "lin_full" or mode == "lin_off":
        L = c.size
        A = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        A[:n] = a
        q = np.real(np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(A, L) * np.conj(np.fft.fft(P, L))))
        grad = 2.0 * q[:n]
    else:
        q = np.real(np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(a, n) * np.conj(np.fft.fft(P, n))))
        grad = 2.0 * q

    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return grad.astype(np.float64, copy=False), m


def gradient_order(a, mode, rng, top_frac=0.7):
    """Create a coordinate order biased by local model gradient magnitude."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int), False
    try:
        grad, _ = smooth_gradient(a, mode, 20.0)
    except Exception:
        return rng.permutation(n), False
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    mags = np.abs(grad * a)
    if float(np.sum(mags)) <= 1e-30:
        return rng.permutation(n), False

    k_top = max(1, int(n * max(0.05, min(0.95, top_frac))))
    if k_top >= n:
        order = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
        rng.shuffle(order)
        return order, True

    top = np.argpartition(mags, -k_top)[-k_top:]
    rest = np.setdiff1d(np.arange(n), top)
    top = top.astype(int, copy=False)
    rest = rest.astype(int, copy=False)
    rng.shuffle(top)
    rng.shuffle(rest)
    order = np.concatenate((top, rest)).astype(int, copy=False)
    return order, True


def noisy_candidate(a, target_sum, rng, scale, scale_inv):
    """Full multiplicative Gaussian perturbation."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a.copy()
    scale = float(np.clip(scale, 1e-4, 2.0))
    noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, scale, n))
    trial = a * noise
    if scale_inv:
        return clean_raw(trial, target_sum)
    return normalize_positive(trial, target_sum)


def build_lp_matrix(a, mode, k_indices):
    """Build sparse matrix for (a * g) on selected lags."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    k_indices = np.asarray(k_indices, dtype=int)
    m_constr = k_indices.size
    if m_constr == 0:
        return csr_matrix((0, n))

    if mode == "lin_full" or mode == "lin_off":
        counts = np.minimum(n - 1, k_indices) - np.maximum(0, k_indices - n + 1) + 1
        counts = np.maximum(counts, 0)
        total = int(counts.sum())
        rows = np.empty(total, dtype=int)
        cols = np.empty(total, dtype=int)
        vals = np.empty(total, dtype=np.float64)
        ptr = 0
        for idx, k in enumerate(k_indices):
            k = int(k)
            lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, k)
            cnt = hi - lo + 1
            if cnt <= 0:
                continue
            js = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=int)
            rows[ptr:ptr + cnt] = idx
            cols[ptr:ptr + cnt] = js
            vals[ptr:ptr + cnt] = a[k - js]
            ptr += cnt
        return csr_matrix((vals, (rows, cols)), shape=(m_constr, n))
    else:
        total = m_constr * n
        rows = np.empty(total, dtype=int)
        cols = np.empty(total, dtype=int)
        vals = np.empty(total, dtype=np.float64)
        ptr = 0
        for idx, k in enumerate(k_indices):
            k = int(k) % n
            js = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
            rows[ptr:ptr + n] = idx
            cols[ptr:ptr + n] = js
            vals[ptr:ptr + n] = a[(k - js) % n]
            ptr += n
        return csr_matrix((vals, (rows, cols)), shape=(m_constr, n))


def select_top_indices(c, mask, top_frac):
    """Select the most active constraints."""
    active = np.where(mask)[0]
    if active.size <= 24 or top_frac >= 1.0:
        return active
    vals = c[active]
    vals = np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 0.0)
    thresh = float(np.percentile(vals, (1.0 - max(0.05, min(0.95, top_frac))) * 100.0))
    sel = active[vals >= thresh]
    if sel.size == 0:
        sel = active[:1]
    return sel


def solve_lp_direction(a, mode, M, top_frac=0.6, time_limit=1.5):
    """Solve max sum(g) s.t. (a*g)_k <= M for selected active lags."""
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or M <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None

    c, mask = get_conv_mask(a, mode)
    if c.size == 0 or not np.any(mask):
        return None
    k_idx = select_top_indices(c, mask, top_frac)
    if k_idx.size == 0:
        return None

    try:
        A = build_lp_matrix(a, mode, k_idx)
    except Exception:
        return None

    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    b_ub = np.full(k_idx.size, float(M), dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": float(max(0.1, time_limit)), "presolve": True}
        )
        if res.success and res.x is not None:
            g = np.maximum(res.x, 0.0)
            g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
            s = float(g.sum())
            if s > 1e-12:
                return g.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def directed_model_search(a0, target_sum, mode, deadline, rng, scale_inv):
    """Model-aware local search: smooth gradient plus limited LP directions."""
    if mode is None:
        return a0, fast_eval_array(a0)

    a = spana = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy() if False else None
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if scale_inv:
        a = clean_raw(a, target_sum)
    else:
        a = normalize_positive(a, target_sum)

    cur = fast_eval_array(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = cur
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best_a, best_score

    tau = 10.0
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    lp_calls = 0
    max_lp = 25

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        improved = False

        try:
            grad, _ = smooth_gradient(a, mode, tau)
        except Exception:
            grad = None

        if grad is not None:
            grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
            gx = grad * a
            gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(gx))
            if gnorm > 1e-14:
                best_trial = None
                best_sc = cur
                for delta in (0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 2.0):
                    logf = np.clip(-delta * gx / gnorm, -50.0, 50.0)
                    trial = a * np.exp(logf)
                    if scale_inv:
                        trial = clean_raw(trial, target_sum)
                    else:
                        trial = normalize_positive(trial, target_sum)
                    sc = fast_eval_array(trial)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best_trial = trial
                if best_trial is not None:
                    a = best_trial
                    cur = best_sc
                    improved = True
                    if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = cur
                        best_a = a.copy()

        if (not improved and it % 5 == 0 and n <= 1024 and HAVE_SCIPY and lp_calls < max_lp):
            lp_calls += 1
            try:
                M = local_max_conv(a, mode)
            except Exception:
                M = 0.0
            if np.isfinite(M) and M > 0.0:
                top_frac = 0.5 if n <= 768 else 0.35
                tl = 1.2 if n <= 768 else 0.7
                g = solve_lp_direction(a, mode, M, top_frac, tl)
                if g is not None:
                    g = normalize_positive(g, target_sum)
                    for tval in (0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9):
                        trial = (1.0 - tval) * a + tval * g
                        if scale_inv:
                            trial = clean_raw(trial, target_sum)
                        else:
                            trial = normalize_positive(trial, target_sum)
                        sc = fast_eval_array(trial)
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur - 1e-12:
                            a = trial
                            cur = sc
                            improved = True
                            if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
                                best_score = cur
                                best_a = a.copy()
                            break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            tau = max(5.0, tau * 0.9)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            tau = min(80.0, tau * 1.1)
            if no_improve % 10 == 0:
                scale = min(1.0, 0.05 * np.sqrt(float(no_improve)))
                trial = noisy_candidate(a, target_sum, rng, scale, scale_inv)
                sc = fast_eval_array(trial)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur - 1e-12:
                    a = trial
                    cur = sc
                    improved = True
                    if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = cur
                        best_a = a.copy()
            if no_improve >= 150:
                break

    return best_a, best_score


def fd_gradient_search(a0, target_sum, scale_inv, te_est, rng, deadline, mode=None):
    """Model-agnostic finite-difference gradient search in log variables."""
    if not scale_inv:
        return a0, fast_eval_array(a0)

    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, fast_eval_array(a)

    ts = float(target_sum) if target_sum is not None and float(target_sum) > 0.0 else 1.0
    min_val = max(1e-12, ts * 1e-9 / float(n))
    a = np.maximum(a, min_val)
    a = clean_raw(a, ts)

    cur = fast_eval_array(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = cur
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best_a, best_score

    last_mag = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if mode is not None:
        try:
            g0, _ = smooth_gradient(a, mode, 20.0)
            mag0 = np.abs(g0 * a)
            mag0 = np.where(np.isfinite(mag0), mag0, 0.0)
            if float(np.sum(mag0)) > 0.0:
                last_mag = np.maximum(last_mag, mag0)
        except Exception:
            pass

    K = int(0.7 / max(2.0 * float(te_est), 1e-6))
    K = max(8, min(n, K))

    h = 1.0e-3
    no_improve = 0
    step_idx = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        step_idx += 1
        if K >= n:
            idxs = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
        else:
            top_count = max(1, int(K * 0.7))
            top_idx = np.argpartition(last_mag, -top_count)[-top_count:]
            rand_idx = rng.choice(n, K - top_count, replace=False)
            idxs = np.unique(np.concatenate((top_idx, rand_idx)))
            if idxs.size > K:
                idxs = idxs[:K]
            idxs = idxs.astype(int, copy=False)

        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        f0 = cur
        plus_f = float(np.exp(h))
        minus_f = float(np.exp(-h))

        for i in idxs:
            val = float(a[i])
            if val <= 1e-300:
                val = min_val
            can_plus = val * plus_f <= 1000.0
            can_minus = val * minus_f >= min_val
            if can_plus and can_minus:
                ap = a.copy()
                am = a.copy()
                ap[i] = val * plus_f
                am[i] = val * minus_f
                fp = fast_eval_array(ap)
                fm = fast_eval_array(am)
                if np.isfinite(fp) and np.isfinite(fm):
                    grad[i] = (fp - fm) / (2.0 * h)
                elif np.isfinite(fp):
                    grad[i] = (fp - f0) / h
                elif np.isfinite(fm):
                    grad[i] = (f0 - fm) / h
            elif can_plus:
                ap = a.copy()
                ap[i] = val * plus_f
                fp = fast_eval_array(ap)
                if np.isfinite(fp):
                    grad[i] = (fp - f0) / h
            elif can_minus:
                am = a.copy()
                am[i] = val * minus_f
                fm = fast_eval_array(am)
                if np.isfinite(fm):
                    grad[i] = (f0 - fm) / h

        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        improved = False

        if gnorm > 1e-14:
            best_trial = None
            best_sc = cur
            for delta in (0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 2.0):
                logf = np.clip(-delta * grad / gnorm, -50.0, 50.0)
                trial = a * np.exp(logf)
                trial = clean_raw(trial, ts)
                sc = fast_eval_array(trial)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_trial = trial
            if best_trial is not None:
                a = best_trial
                cur = best_sc
                improved = True
                if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = cur
                    best_a = a.copy()

        last_mag = 0.85 * last_mag
        last_mag[idxs] = np.maximum(last_mag[idxs], np.abs(grad[idxs]))

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            h = max(1.0e-3, h * 0.9)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 10 == 0:
                h = min(0.05, h * 1.25)
            if no_improve in (5, 15, 40, 100, 200):
                scale = min(1.0, 0.05 * np.sqrt(float(no_improve)))
                trial = noisy_candidate(a, ts, rng, scale, True)
                sc = fast_eval_array(trial)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur - 1e-12:
                    a = trial
                    cur = sc
                    improved = True
                    if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = cur
                        best_a = a.copy()
            if no_improve >= 200:
                a = best_a.copy()
                cur = best_score
                no_improve = 0

        s = float(a.sum())
        upper = max(100.0, 10.0 * ts)
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01 or s > upper:
            a = normalize_positive(a, ts)
            sc = fast_eval_array(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur:
                cur = sc
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def stochastic_coordinate_search(a0, target_sum, scale_inv, te_est, rng, deadline,
                                 mode=None, model_order_allowed=False, polish=False):
    """Stochastic coordinate/multiplicative local search using the evaluator."""
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if scale_inv:
        a = clean_raw(a, target_sum)
    else:
        a = normalize_positive(a, target_sum)

    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf

    cur = fast_eval_array(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = cur
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return a, np.inf

    if polish:
        factors = (0.0, 0.5, 0.75, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0)
    elif float(te_est) < 0.001:
        factors = (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0, 4.0)
    elif float(te_est) < 0.005:
        factors = (0.0, 0.5, 1.5, 3.0)
    else:
        factors = (0.0, 0.5, 2.0)

    noise_scale = 0.05
    no_improve = 0
    pass_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        pass_count += 1

        if model_order_allowed and mode is not None and pass_count % 3 == 0:
            order, ok = gradient_order(a, mode, rng, 0.7)
            if not ok:
                model_order_allowed = False
        else:
            order = rng.permutation(n)

        pass_improved = False
        current_sum = float(a.sum())
        typ = current_sum / float(n) if current_sum > 0.0 else max(float(target_sum) / float(n), 1e-6)

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break

            old = float(a[i])
            vals = []
            if old <= max(1e-12, 1e-4 * typ):
                vals.extend((0.0, 0.1 * typ, typ, 3.0 * typ, 10.0 * typ))
            else:
                for f in factors:
                    vals.append(old * float(f))

            if not polish:
                rs = max(0.05, noise_scale)
                vals.append(old * float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, min(1.5, rs)))))
                vals.append(old * float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, min(1.5, rs)))))

            best_score_coord = cur
            best_trial = None
            seen = []

            for val in vals:
                val = float(val)
                if not np.isfinite(val) or val < 0.0 or val > 1000.0:
                    continue
                if abs(val - old) <= 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(old)):
                    continue
                duplicate = False
                for sv in seen:
                    if abs(val - sv) <= 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(val)):
                        duplicate = True
                        break
                if duplicate:
                    continue
                seen.append(val)

                trial = a.copy()
                trial[i] = val
                if scale_inv:
                    trial = clean_raw(trial, target_sum)
                else:
                    trial = normalize_positive(trial, target_sum)

                sc = fast_eval_array(trial)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score_coord - 1e-12:
                    best_score_coord = sc
                    best_trial = trial

            if best_trial is not None:
                a = best_trial
                cur = best_score_coord
                pass_improved = True
                if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = cur
                    best_a = a.copy()

        if pass_improved:
            no_improve = 0
            noise_scale = max(0.02, noise_scale * 0.85)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            scale = min(1.5, noise_scale * (1.0 + 0.1 * float(no_improve)))
            trial = noisy_candidate(a, target_sum, rng, scale, scale_inv)
            sc = fast_eval_array(trial)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur - 1e-12:
                a = trial
                cur = sc
                if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = cur
                    best_a = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                noise_scale = min(1.5, noise_scale * 1.15)
                if no_improve >= 8:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    cur = best_score
                    no_improve = 0

        if scale_inv and pass_count % 10 == 0:
            s = float(a.sum())
            upper = max(100.0, 10.0 * float(target_sum) if target_sum and float(target_sum) > 0 else 100.0)
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01 or s > upper:
                a = normalize_positive(a, target_sum)
                sc = fast_eval_array(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur:
                    cur = sc
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def explore_lengths(a, score, target_sum, scale_inv, te_est, rng, deadline, mode, model_order_allowed):
    """Try nearby lengths and briefly optimize improved valid lengths."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    score = float(score) if np.isfinite(score) else np.inf
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    n = best_a.size
    if n < 8 or n > 100000 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return best_a, best_score

    ns = []
    raw_ns = (n * 2, n + 128, max(64, n - 128), n // 2, 256, 512, 768, 1024, 1536)
    for nn in raw_ns:
        nn = int(nn)
        if nn < 16 or nn > 4096 or nn == n:
            continue
        duplicate = False
        for old in ns:
            if old == nn:
                duplicate = True
                break
        if not duplicate:
            ns.append(nn)

    for nn in ns:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        try:
            a_new = resample_positive(best_a, nn)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if a_new.size != nn or float(a_new.sum()) < 0.01:
            continue

        te_len = float(te_est) * (float(nn) / float(n)) ** 1.8
        te_len = max(1e-5, te_len)

        if scale_inv:
            a_try = normalize_positive(a_new, target_sum)
            sc = fast_eval_array(a_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a_try.copy()
                add = min(120.0, max(20.0, 0.4 * max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic())))
                dl = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + add)
                if dl > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
                    best_a, best_score = stochastic_coordinate_search(
                        best_a, target_sum, scale_inv, te_len, rng, dl, mode, model_order_allowed, False
                    )
        else:
            ts_list = []
            raw_ts = (target_sum, max(0.03, float(target_sum) * nn / float(n)), max(0.03, float(target_sum)))
            for ts in raw_ts:
                duplicate = False
                for old in ts_list:
                    if abs(old - ts) <= 1e-8 * max(1.0, abs(old)):
                        duplicate = True
                        break
                if not duplicate:
                    ts_list.append(float(ts))
            for ts in ts_list:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break
                a_try = normalize_positive(a_new, ts)
                sc = fast_eval_array(a_try)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a_try.copy()
                    add = min(80.0, max(15.0, 0.3 * max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic())))
                    dl = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + add)
                    if dl > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
                        best_a, best_score = stochastic_coordinate_search(
                            best_a, ts, scale_inv, te_len, rng, dl, mode, model_order_allowed, False
                        )

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    """Main entry point: search within the time budget and return best sequence."""
    if budget_s is None:
        budget = 1500.0
    else:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget < 60.0:
        budget = 60.0

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + budget - 20.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    MAX_SEARCH_N = 1536
    parent_original = load_parent_sequence()
    fallback_a = None
    fallback_score = np.inf

    if parent_original is not None:
        if parent_original.size > MAX_SEARCH_N:
            fallback_a = parent_original.copy()
            fallback_score = fast_eval_array(fallback_a)
            search_base = resample_positive(parent_original, MAX_SEARCH_N)
        else:
            search_base = parent_original.copy()
    else:
        search_base = make_initial_sequence(512, rng)

    if search_base.size == 0 or float(search_base.sum()) < 0.01:
        search_base = make_initial_sequence(512, rng)

    best_a, best_score, target_sum, scale_inv = choose_scale_and_check(search_base)

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = make_initial_sequence(512, rng)
        best_score = fast_eval_array(best_a)
        target_sum = 1.0
        scale_inv = True

    try:
        mode, mode_diff, local_best = infer_mode(best_a, best_score)
    except Exception:
        mode, mode_diff, local_best = "lin_full", np.inf, np.inf

    model_ok = (np.isfinite(best_score) and np.isfinite(mode_diff) and mode_diff < 1e-2)
    model_order_allowed = (np.isfinite(best_score) and np.isfinite(mode_diff) and mode_diff < 0.25)

    te = estimate_eval_time(best_a, 3)

    print(f"START n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.8f} target_sum={target_sum:.6f} "
          f"scale_inv={scale_inv} mode={mode} model_ok={model_ok} te={te:.6f}s", flush=True)

    now = time.monotonic()
    if model_ok and now + 30.0 < deadline:
        dm_end = min(deadline - 350.0, now + min(180.0, 0.15 * budget))
        if dm_end > now + 20.0:
            best_a, best_score = directed_model_search(
                best_a, target_sum, mode, dm_end, rng, scale_inv
            )
            print(f"after directed_model n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.8f} "
                  f"t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    now = time.monotonic()
    if scale_inv and now + 30.0 < deadline:
        fd_end = min(deadline - 300.0, now + min(600.0, 0.40 * budget))
        if fd_end > now + 20.0:
            best_a, best_score = fd_gradient_search(
                best_a, target_sum, scale_inv, te, rng, fd_end, mode
            )
            print(f"after fd_gradient n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.8f} "
                  f"t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    now = time.monotonic()
    coord_end = min(deadline - 180.0, now + min(1000.0, 0.55 * budget))
    if coord_end > now + 10.0:
        best_a, best_score = stochastic_coordinate_search(
            best_a, target_sum, scale_inv, te, rng, coord_end,
            mode, model_order_allowed, False
        )
        print(f"after coordinate n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.8f} "
              f"t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    now = time.monotonic()
    length_end = min(deadline - 120.0, now + min(180.0, 0.12 * budget))
    if length_end > now + 20.0:
        best_a, best_score = explore_lengths(
            best_a, best_score, target_sum, scale_inv, te, rng, length_end,
            mode, model_order_allowed
        )
        print(f"after lengths n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.8f} "
              f"t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now + 25.0 < deadline:
        te = estimate_eval_time(best_a, 3)

    final_end = deadline - 5.0
    if final_end > now + 10.0:
        best_a, best_score = stochastic_coordinate_search(
            best_a, target_sum, scale_inv, te, rng, final_end,
            mode, model_order_allowed, True
        )
        print(f"after polish n={best_a.size} score={best_score:.8f} "
              f"t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    if fallback_a is not None and np.isfinite(fallback_score):
        if (not np.isfinite(best_score) or fallback_score < best_score):
            best_a = fallback_a.copy()
            best_score = fallback_score

    final_a = clean_output(best_a)
    final_score = safe_eval_array(final_a)

    if fallback_a is not None and np.isfinite(fallback_score):
        if (not np.isfinite(final_score) or fallback_score < final_score):
            final_a = clean_output(fallback_a)
            final_score = fallback_score

    print(f"FINAL n={final_a.size} score={final_score:.8f} "
          f"time={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    return [float(x) for x in final_a]
```
